# Ascendance 3.36.21 mechanics audit

This branch repairs the confirmed audit defects and implements the ten previously unsupported items. See IMPLEMENTED_RULES.md for reviewed/default rule definitions. Full effect certification remains incomplete. The downtime, Snare and reward follow-up is documented in DOWNTIME_SNARE_33622.md; the ordinary-action Gold rollback correction is in RESOURCE_ROLLBACK_33623.md. Regional deck, Blight Spores, and location-layout corrections are documented in ENCOUNTERS_LAYOUT_33624.md. Enemy-phase refresh and Search Action fixes are documented in PHASE_SEARCH_SYNC_33625.md. Atomic next-Quest setup, Lost Hero replacement, Encounter prompts and combat Reputation follow-up are documented in QUEST_TRANSITIONS_33626.md. Activation status effects, selected card effects, purchases, armory and World Event advancement are documented in WEBSITE_EFFECTS_33627.md. Gameplay tests did not mutate production or staging player data. All functional requests target a local server with a new temporary persistence directory and isolated browser contexts. Deployment status is recorded separately from these isolated tests.

## Reproduction

Node 24; npm ci; npx playwright install --with-deps chromium; npm run audit:mechanics. This cloud run uses the bundled Playwright Chromium (PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH=/workspace/.cache/ms-playwright, CHROMIUM_PATH unset). npm test runs the new checked-in foundation simulator. npm run stability:33621 runs the full browser suite. These are explicit replacements; the original historical suites were not recovered. The strict effect coverage gate remains separate from scenario pass/fail results.

## Current execution

{"passed":420}

Every passing row certifies only its named assertions. XP thresholds are not ability activation coverage; resolver tests are not full card coverage; dispatcher observations are not passes. Exceptions from generic fixtures need reproduction in valid gameplay before they become confirmed card defects.

Spreading Misery — Once per Round after an afflicted Enemy is defeated, Veyra may give one harmful Status Effect it had to another Enemy in the same Location. Queen of Curses — Once per Quest, choose a Location within 2 connections. Each Enemy there gains Weakened and Marked until the end of its next activation. Hexmaster — The first time each Round Veyra applies a harmful Status Effect to an Enemy, that Enemy also becomes Marked until the end of its next activation. Curse Cascade — Once per Quest, choose Veyra's Location or a connected revealed Location. Doomweaver — Once per Round when Veyra applies a harmful Status Effect to an Enemy, choose another Enemy in that Location. Apply the same Status to that Enemy if it is a legal recipient. Malison Eternal — Once per Quest, spend 1 Action. Choose up to 3 Enemies in Veyra's Location or a connected Location. Master of Ruin — The first time each Round Veyra damages an Enemy with a harmful Status Effect, spread one of that Enemy's harmful Status Effects to another legal Enemy in the same Location. Curse of the Last Name — Once per Quest, choose one Enemy. Until it is defeated, it suffers -2 Armor and cannot remove harmful Status Effects except through Boss/Quest immunity rules. When Veyra applies a harmful Status, once per Round she may apply a weaker or shorter copy of it to another Enemy in the same Location. ## Remaining coverage gaps

Ability effects at levels 1–16, every branch/trigger/expiry of all 323 cards, all equipment and loot effects, full Enemy AI and targeting matrix, all campaign quests, concurrent reaction timing, and adversarial synchronization remain unverified except for explicit named assertions above. The generic dispatcher sweep must not be substituted for those tests. Staging was not used for mutations. Local execution is recorded here; hosted workflow and deployment outcomes are reported separately. A new-task environment restore remains unverified.
